// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// BUILD DOM
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function buildAll() {
  buildSecTabs();
  buildAttemptCards();
  buildSidePanel();
  buildSolQRow();
  buildSolSecCircles();
  buildViewCards();
  goToQ(1);
  startTimer();
}

function buildSecTabs() {
  var t = document.getElementById('sec-tabs');
  t.innerHTML = '';
  SEC_NAMES.forEach(function(name, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'stab' + (i===0?' active':'');
    b.textContent = name;
    b.onclick = function(){ secTabClick(i); };
    t.appendChild(b);
  });
}

function buildAttemptCards() {
  var body = document.getElementById('attempt-body');
  body.innerHTML = '';
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'qcard-attempt';
    div.id = 'acard' + q.n;
    div.innerHTML = buildAttemptCard(q);
    body.appendChild(div);
  });
}

function buildAttemptCard(q) {
  var pos = q.pos || ''; var neg = q.neg || '';
  var badges = '';
  if(pos) badges += '<span class="badge-pos">+ ' + pos + '</span>';
  if(neg) badges += '<span class="badge-neg">- ' + neg + '</span>';

  var comp = q.comp ? '<div class="passage">' + q.comp + '</div>' : '';

  var opts = '';
  q.opts.forEach(function(ot, oi) {
    opts += '<div class="opt-a" id="opta_' + q.n + '_' + oi + '" onclick="selectOpt(' + q.n + ',' + oi + ')">' +
      '<span class="opt-num">' + (oi+1) + '.</span>' +
      '<span class="opt-body-a">' + ot + '</span>' +
      '<span class="opt-icon" id="oicon_' + q.n + '_' + oi + '"></span>' +
      '</div>';
  });

  return '<div class="qrow-hdr">' +
    '<div class="qnum-circle" id="qnc' + q.n + '">' + q.n + '</div>' +
    '<div class="qtimer"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"/></svg><span id="qt' + q.n + '">00:00</span></div>' +
    badges +
    '<div class="qicons">' +
      '<button class="qicon-btn" id="qibookmark' + q.n + '" onclick="toggleBookmark(' + q.n + ',event)" title="Bookmark">🔖</button>' +
      '<button class="qicon-btn" id="qistar' + q.n + '" onclick="toggleStar(' + q.n + ',event)" title="Mark">☆</button>' +
    '</div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="qcard-body-a">' +
    comp +
    '<div class="qtext">' + q.text + '</div>' +
    '<div class="opts-wrap">' + opts + '</div>' +
    '</div>';
}

function buildSidePanel() {
  var sp = document.getElementById('sp-sections');
  sp.innerHTML = '';
  SEC_NAMES.forEach(function(name, si) {
    var block = document.createElement('div');
    block.className = 'sp-sec-block';
    block.id = 'spblock' + si;
    block.innerHTML =
      '<div class="sp-sec-hdr" onclick="toggleSpSec(' + si + ')">' +
        '<span>' + name + '</span>' +
        '<span id="sp-arrow' + si + '">∧</span>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="sp-sec-stats" id="spstats' + si + '"></div>' +
      '<div class="sp-pal" id="sppal' + si + '"></div>';
    sp.appendChild(block);
  });
  // Build palette buttons
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'pal-btn';
    btn.id = 'palbtn' + q.n;
    btn.textContent = q.n;
    btn.onclick = function(){ goToQ(q.n); closePanel(); };
    document.getElementById('sppal' + q.si).appendChild(btn);
  });
  refreshPalette();
}

function buildSolQRow() {
  var row = document.getElementById('sol-qrow');
  row.innerHTML = '';
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'sol-qbtn';
    btn.id = 'sqbtn' + q.n;
    btn.textContent = q.n;
    btn.onclick = function(){ solScrollToQ(q.n); };
    row.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

function buildViewCards() {
  var body = document.getElementById('view-body');
  body.innerHTML = '';
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'sol-card';
    div.id = 'vcard' + q.n;
    div.innerHTML = buildViewCard(q);
    body.appendChild(div);
  });
}

function buildViewCard(q) {
  var pos = q.pos||''; var neg = q.neg||'';
  var badges = '';

  var myAns   = answers[String(q.n)];
  var correct = q.cidx;

  // Status badge — show whether the user skipped / got it right / got it wrong
  if(myAns === -1 || myAns === undefined) {
    badges += '<span class="status-badge status-skip">⏭ Skipped</span>';
  } else if(myAns === correct) {
    badges += '<span class="status-badge status-ok">✓ Correct</span>';
  } else {
    badges += '<span class="status-badge status-err">✗ Incorrect</span>';
  }

  if(pos) badges += '<span class="badge-pos">+ '+pos+'</span>';
  if(neg) badges += '<span class="badge-neg">- '+neg+'</span>';

  var comp = q.comp ? '<div class="passage">'+q.comp+'</div>' : '';

  var opts = '';
  q.opts.forEach(function(ot, oi) {
    var cls = 'opt-sol';
    var icon = '';
    var youLabel = '';
    var isCorrect = (oi === correct);
    var isMine    = (myAns !== -1 && myAns !== undefined && oi === myAns);

    if(isCorrect) {
      cls += ' sol-correct';
      icon = '<span class="opt-sol-icon" style="color:#4caf50;font-size:18px">✓</span>';
    }
    if(isMine && !isCorrect) {
      cls += ' sol-wrong-sel';
      icon = '<span class="opt-sol-icon" style="color:#f44336;font-size:18px">✗</span>';
    }
    if(isMine) {
      youLabel = '<span class="your-answer-badge">Your Answer</span>';
    }

    opts += '<div class="' + cls + '">' +
      '<span class="opt-sol-num">' + (oi+1) + '.</span>' +
      '<span class="opt-sol-body">' + ot + '</span>' +
      youLabel +
      icon +
      '</div>';
  });

  var correctAns = correct + 1;
  var pctHtml = '<span class="correct-pct">-- got this right</span>';

  var solHtml = '';
  if(q.sol) {
    solHtml = '<div class="solution-section">' +
      '<div class="sol-heading">Solution</div>' +
      '<div class="sol-text">' + q.sol + '</div>' +
      '</div>';
  }

  var timeSpent = qTimes[String(q.n)] || 0;
  var ts = timeSpent < 3 ? timeSpent + 'sec' : Math.floor(timeSpent/60) ? Math.floor(timeSpent/60)+'min '+( timeSpent%60)+'sec' : timeSpent+'sec';

  return '<div class="sol-card-hdr">' +
    '<div class="qnum-circle ' + stateClass(q.n) + '" style="width:28px;height:28px;font-size:12px">' + q.n + '</div>' +
    '<div class="qtimer"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"/></svg>' + ts + '</div>' +
    badges +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="sol-card-body">' +
    comp +
    '<div class="qtext">' + q.text + '</div>' +
    '<div class="opts-wrap" style="margin-top:14px">' + opts + '</div>' +
    '<div class="correct-bar"><span>Correct Answers Is: ' + correctAns + '</span>' + pctHtml + '</div>' +
    solHtml +
    '</div>';
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// NAVIGATION
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function goToQ(n) {
  // save time for previous question
  if(curQ && curMode === 'attempt') {
    qTimes[String(curQ)] += Math.round((Date.now() - qStartTime) / 1000);
    qVisited[String(curQ)] = true;
    // update per-question timer display
    var ts = qTimes[String(curQ)];
    var el = document.getElementById('qt' + curQ);
    if(el) el.textContent = (ts >= 60 ? Math.floor(ts/60)+':'+pad(ts%60) : '00:'+pad(ts));
  }
  // hide previous card
  var prevCard = document.getElementById('acard' + curQ);
  if(prevCard) prevCard.classList.remove('active-card');

  qStartTime = Date.now();
  curQ = n;
  qVisited[String(n)] = true;

  var q = Q_DATA[n-1];
  curSec = q.si;
  // update sec tabs
  document.querySelectorAll('.stab').forEach(function(b,i){
    b.classList.toggle('active', i === curSec);
  });
  // show current card only
  var card = document.getElementById('acard' + n);
  if(card) {
    card.classList.add('active-card');
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }
  refreshPalette();
}

function secTabClick(si) {
  curSec = si;
  document.querySelectorAll('.stab').forEach(function(b,i){
    b.classList.toggle('active', i===si);
  });
  // go to first q of this section
  if(SEC_QNUMS[si] && SEC_QNUMS[si].length) goToQ(SEC_QNUMS[si][0]);
}

function saveAndNext() {
  // just advance
  nextQ();
}

function markAndNext() {
  marked[String(curQ)] = true;
  refreshPalette();
  updateQCircle(curQ);
  nextQ();
}

function clearCurrent() {
  answers[String(curQ)] = -1;
  marked[String(curQ)] = false;
  renderOpts(curQ);
  updateQCircle(curQ);
  refreshPalette();
}

function nextQ() {
  if(curQ < TOTAL) goToQ(curQ + 1);
}

function prevQ() {
  if(curQ > 1) goToQ(curQ - 1);
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// OPTION SELECT
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function selectOpt(qn, oi) {
  if(submitted && !reattempt) return;
  if(submitted && reattempt) {
    // reattempt mode: allow re-selection
    answers[String(qn)] = oi;
    renderOpts(qn);
    return;
  }
  // toggle
  if(answers[String(qn)] === oi) {
    answers[String(qn)] = -1;
  } else {
    answers[String(qn)] = oi;
  }
  renderOpts(qn);
  updateQCircle(qn);
  refreshPalette();
}

function renderOpts(qn) {
  var q = Q_DATA[qn-1];
  q.opts.forEach(function(_, oi) {
    var el = document.getElementById('opta_' + qn + '_' + oi);
    if(!el) return;
    el.classList.remove('sel','reveal-correct','reveal-wrong');
    var icon = document.getElementById('oicon_' + qn + '_' + oi);
    if(icon) icon.innerHTML = '';

    if(submitted && !reattempt) {
      if(oi === q.cidx) {
        el.classList.add('reveal-correct');
        if(icon) icon.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#4caf50;font-size:18px">✓</span>';
      } else if(oi === answers[String(qn)] && answers[String(qn)] !== q.cidx) {
        el.classList.add('reveal-wrong');
        if(icon) icon.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#f44336">✗ <span style="color:#aaa;font-size:12px">○</span> <span style="color:#4caf50">↺</span></span>';
      }
    } else if(answers[String(qn)] === oi) {
      el.classList.add('sel');
    }
  });
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// Q CIRCLE / PALETTE
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function stateClass(qn) {
  if(submitted) {
    var ans = answers[String(qn)];
    var correct = Q_MAP[String(qn)].correct;
    if(ans === -1) return '';
    return ans === correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong';
  }
  if(marked[String(qn)]) return 'marked';
  if(answers[String(qn)] !== -1) return 'attempted';
  return '';
}

function updateQCircle(qn) {
  var el = document.getElementById('qnc' + qn);
  if(!el) return;
  el.className = 'qnum-circle ' + stateClass(qn);
}

function refreshPalette() {
  // update palette buttons
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var btn = document.getElementById('palbtn' + q.n);
    if(!btn) return;
    btn.className = 'pal-btn';
    var sc = stateClass(q.n);
    if(sc === 'attempted') btn.classList.add('p-attempted');
    else if(sc === 'marked') btn.classList.add('p-marked');
    else if(sc === 'correct') btn.classList.add('p-correct');
    else if(sc === 'wrong') btn.classList.add('p-wrong');
    if(q.n === curQ) btn.classList.add('p-current');
  });
  // update section stats
  SEC_NAMES.forEach(function(_, si) {
    var qns = SEC_QNUMS[si];
    var att=0, mrk=0, unatt=0, unseen=0;
    qns.forEach(function(qn) {
      if(marked[String(qn)]) mrk++;
      else if(answers[String(qn)] !== -1) att++;
      else if(!qVisited[String(qn)]) unseen++;
      else unatt++;
    });
    var el = document.getElementById('spstats' + si);
    if(el) el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="sp-stat"><div class="dot" style="background:#f44336"></div><span style="color:#f44336">★ '+mrk+'</span></div>' +
      '<div class="sp-stat"><div class="dot" style="background:#2196f3"></div><span>'+att+'</span></div>' +
      '<div class="sp-stat"><div class="dot" style="background:#bbb"></div><span>'+unatt+'</span></div>' +
      '<div class="sp-stat"><div class="dot" style="border:1.5px solid #ccc;background:#fff"></div><span>'+unseen+'</span></div>';
  });
  // update q circles for all
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) { updateQCircle(q.n); });
}

function toggleSpSec(si) {
  var pal = document.getElementById('sppal'+si);
  var arrow = document.getElementById('sp-arrow'+si);
  if(!pal) return;
  if(pal.style.display === 'none') {
    pal.style.display = '';
    if(arrow) arrow.textContent = '∧';
  } else {
    pal.style.display = 'none';
    if(arrow) arrow.textContent = '∨';
  }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// BOOKMARK / STAR
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function toggleBookmark(qn, e) {
  e.stopPropagation();
  var btn = document.getElementById('qibookmark'+qn);
  if(!btn) return;
  btn.classList.toggle('bookmark-on');
}
function toggleStar(qn, e) {
  e.stopPropagation();
  marked[String(qn)] = !marked[String(qn)];
  var btn = document.getElementById('qistar'+qn);
  if(btn) { btn.classList.toggle('star-on', marked[String(qn)]); btn.textContent = marked[String(qn)] ? '★' : '☆'; }
  updateQCircle(qn);
  refreshPalette();
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// PANEL
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function openPanel() {
  document.getElementById('side-overlay').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('side-panel').classList.add('open');
  refreshPalette();
}
function closePanel() {
  document.getElementById('side-overlay').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('side-panel').classList.remove('open');
}
function spTab(i) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.sp-tab').forEach(function(b,idx){ b.classList.toggle('active',idx===i); });
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// SUBMIT
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function openSubmitModal() {
  var att=0, mrk=0;
  Object.keys(answers).forEach(function(k){ if(answers[k]!==-1) att++; if(marked[k]) mrk++; });
  document.getElementById('modal-attempted').textContent    = att;
  document.getElementById('modal-unattempted').textContent  = TOTAL - att;
  document.getElementById('modal-marked').textContent       = mrk;
  document.getElementById('modal-timer').textContent        = document.getElementById('hdr-timer').textContent;
  document.getElementById('submit-modal').classList.add('show');
  closePanel();
}
function closeSubmitModal() {
  document.getElementById('submit-modal').classList.remove('show');
}
function confirmSubmit() {
  closeSubmitModal();
  submitted = true;
  clearInterval(timerInterval);
  // reveal all opts in attempt mode
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) { renderOpts(q.n); });
  refreshPalette();
  // show analysis page
  showAnalysis();
}

function normCdf(z) {
  // Abramowitz-Stegun approximation of the standard normal CDF.
  var t = 1/(1+0.2316419*Math.abs(z));
  var d = 0.3989423*Math.exp(-z*z/2);
  var p = d*t*(0.3193815+t*(-0.3565638+t*(1.781478+t*(-1.821256+t*1.330274))));
  return z>0 ? 1-p : p;
}

function renderRank(score, maxScore) {
  var card = document.getElementById('ana-rank-card');
  var A = ANALYSIS || {};
  var attempts = A.attempts || 0;
  var maxMarks = A.maxMarks || maxScore || 0;
  var topper   = A.topperMarks || 0;
  var avg      = A.avgMarks;
  // Nothing useful to estimate from → hide the card entirely.
  if(!attempts && (avg===undefined||avg===null) && !topper) { card.style.display='none'; return; }
  // Approximate the score distribution as a normal curve.
  if(avg===undefined||avg===null) avg = topper ? topper*0.45 : maxMarks*0.35;
  var std = (topper && topper>avg) ? (topper-avg)/3 : Math.max(1,(maxMarks||1)*0.15);
  if(std<=0) std = Math.max(1,(maxMarks||1)*0.15);
  var z = (score-avg)/std;
  var pct = normCdf(z)*100;
  if(topper && score>=topper) pct = 99.9;
  pct = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(99.9, pct));
  document.getElementById('ana-percentile').textContent = pct.toFixed(1) + ' %ile';
  if(attempts) {
    var rank = Math.max(1, Math.round((1-pct/100)*attempts));
    if(topper && score>=topper) rank = 1;
    document.getElementById('ana-rank').textContent = '#' + rank.toLocaleString();
    document.getElementById('ana-attempts').textContent = attempts.toLocaleString();
  } else {
    document.getElementById('ana-rank').textContent = '-';
    document.getElementById('ana-attempts').textContent = '-';
  }
  var meta = [];
  if(topper) meta.push('Topper: ' + (topper%1===0?topper:topper.toFixed(1)) + ' / ' + maxMarks);
  if(A.avgMarks!==undefined && A.avgMarks!==null) meta.push('Avg: ' + (A.avgMarks%1===0?A.avgMarks:A.avgMarks.toFixed(1)));
  if(A.cutoff) meta.push('Cutoff: ' + A.cutoff);
  meta.push('Estimated from Testbook attempt data — actual rank may vary.');
  document.getElementById('ana-rank-meta').innerHTML = meta.join(' &nbsp;•&nbsp; ');
  card.style.display = 'block';
}

function showAnalysis() {
  var totalCorrect=0, totalWrong=0, totalSkip=0;
  var totalScore=0, maxScore=0;
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var ans = answers[String(q.n)];
    var pos = Q_MAP[String(q.n)].pos || 1;
    var neg = Q_MAP[String(q.n)].neg || 0;
    maxScore += pos;
    if(ans === -1) { totalSkip++; }
    else if(ans === q.cidx) { totalCorrect++; totalScore += pos; }
    else { totalWrong++; totalScore -= neg; }
  });
  var attempted = totalCorrect + totalWrong;
  var accuracy = attempted > 0 ? Math.round((totalCorrect / attempted) * 100) : 0;

  document.getElementById('ana-score').textContent = totalScore % 1 === 0 ? totalScore : totalScore.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('ana-max').textContent = maxScore % 1 === 0 ? maxScore : maxScore.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('ana-accuracy').textContent = accuracy + ' %';
  document.getElementById('ana-attempted').textContent = attempted + '/' + TOTAL;
  document.getElementById('ana-correct-count').textContent = totalCorrect;
  document.getElementById('ana-wrong-count').textContent = totalWrong;

  // Estimated rank & percentile (only when Testbook attempt stats exist)
  renderRank(totalScore, maxScore);

  // Question analysis grid
  document.getElementById('ana-grid').innerHTML =
    '<div class="analysis-grid-item ag-correct"><div class="analysis-grid-val">' + totalCorrect + '</div><div class="analysis-grid-label">Correct</div></div>' +
    '<div class="analysis-grid-item ag-wrong"><div class="analysis-grid-val">' + totalWrong + '</div><div class="analysis-grid-label">Incorrect</div></div>' +
    '<div class="analysis-grid-item ag-skip"><div class="analysis-grid-val">' + totalSkip + '</div><div class="analysis-grid-label">Unattempted</div></div>' +
    '<div class="analysis-grid-item ag-total"><div class="analysis-grid-val">' + TOTAL + '</div><div class="analysis-grid-label">Total Questions</div></div>';

  // Section-wise breakdown
  var secHtml = '';
  SEC_NAMES.forEach(function(name, si) {
    var qns = SEC_QNUMS[si];
    var sc=0, wc=0, sk=0, secScore=0, secMax=0;
    qns.forEach(function(qn) {
      var ans = answers[String(qn)];
      var pos = Q_MAP[String(qn)].pos || 1;
      var neg = Q_MAP[String(qn)].neg || 0;
      secMax += pos;
      if(ans === -1) sk++;
      else if(ans === Q_MAP[String(qn)].correct) { sc++; secScore += pos; }
      else { wc++; secScore -= neg; }
    });
    var pct = secMax > 0 ? Math.round(Math.max(0, secScore) / secMax * 100) : 0;
    secHtml += '<div class="analysis-bar-row">' +
      '<span class="analysis-bar-label">' + name + '</span>' +
      '<div class="analysis-bar-track"><div class="analysis-bar-fill" style="width:' + pct + '%;background:' + (pct >= 50 ? '#4caf50' : '#f44336') + '"></div></div>' +
      '<span class="analysis-bar-val">' + (secScore % 1 === 0 ? secScore : secScore.toFixed(1)) + '/' + secMax + '</span>' +
      '</div>';
  });
  document.getElementById('ana-sections').innerHTML = secHtml;

  switchMode('analysis');
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// MODE SWITCH
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function switchMode(mode) {
  curMode = mode;
  var isAttempt = mode === 'attempt';
  var isView = mode === 'view';
  var isAnalysis = mode === 'analysis';
  document.getElementById('hdr-attempt').style.display = isAttempt ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('hdr-view').style.display    = isView ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('mode-attempt').style.display = isAttempt ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('mode-view').style.display    = isView ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('mode-analysis').style.display = isAnalysis ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('attempt-bar').style.display  = isAttempt ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('sol-bar').style.display      = isView ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('sec-tabs').style.display     = isAnalysis ? 'none' : 'flex';
  if(isView) {
    // rebuild view cards with current answer state
    buildViewCards();
    refreshSolQRow();
    solScrollToQ(1);
  }
}

function refreshSolQRow() {
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var btn = document.getElementById('sqbtn' + q.n);
    if(!btn) return;
    btn.className = 'sol-qbtn';
    var ans = answers[String(q.n)];
    if(ans === -1) btn.classList.add('sq-skip');
    else if(ans === q.cidx) btn.classList.add('sq-correct');
    else btn.classList.add('sq-wrong');
  });
}

function solScrollToQ(n) {
  curQ = n;
  var card = document.getElementById('vcard' + n);
  if(card) card.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'});
}

function solNav(dir) {
  var newQ = curQ + dir;
  if(newQ < 1) newQ = 1;
  if(newQ > TOTAL) newQ = TOTAL;
  solScrollToQ(newQ);
  document.getElementById('btn-prev').disabled = (newQ <= 1);
  document.getElementById('btn-next').disabled = (newQ >= TOTAL);
}

function buildSolSecCircles() {
  var container = document.getElementById('sol-sec-circles');
  if(!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';
  var allBtn = document.createElement('button');
  allBtn.className = 'sol-sec-circle sec-all sec-active';
  allBtn.textContent = 'All';
  allBtn.onclick = function() { filterSolSec(-1); };
  allBtn.id = 'sol-sec-all';
  container.appendChild(allBtn);
  SEC_NAMES.forEach(function(name, i) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'sol-sec-circle';
    btn.textContent = (i + 1);
    btn.title = name;
    btn.id = 'sol-sec-btn' + i;
    btn.onclick = function() { filterSolSec(i); };
    container.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

function filterSolSec(si) {
  solFilter = si;
  var allBtn = document.getElementById('sol-sec-all');
  if(allBtn) allBtn.classList.toggle('sec-active', si === -1);
  SEC_NAMES.forEach(function(_, i) {
    var btn = document.getElementById('sol-sec-btn' + i);
    if(btn) btn.classList.toggle('sec-active', i === si);
  });
  Q_DATA.forEach(function(q) {
    var card = document.getElementById('vcard' + q.n);
    var qbtn = document.getElementById('sqbtn' + q.n);
    var show = (si === -1 || q.si === si);
    if(card) card.style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
    if(qbtn) qbtn.style.display = show ? 'flex' : 'none';
  });
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// REATTEMPT TOGGLE
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function toggleReattempt() {
  reattempt = !reattempt;
  var track = document.getElementById('rt-track');
  var thumb = document.getElementById('rt-thumb');
  track.classList.toggle('off', !reattempt);
  thumb.classList.toggle('on', reattempt);
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// TIMER
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function startTimer() {
  timerInterval = setInterval(function() {
    if(paused) return;
    timerSecs--;
    if(timerSecs <= 0) { timerSecs = 0; clearInterval(timerInterval); confirmSubmit(); }
    var h = Math.floor(timerSecs/3600);
    var m = Math.floor((timerSecs%3600)/60);
    var s = timerSecs%60;
    var str = pad(h)+':'+pad(m)+':'+pad(s);
    document.getElementById('hdr-timer').textContent = str;
    document.getElementById('modal-timer').textContent = str;
  }, 1000);
}
function pad(n){ return n<10?'0'+n:n; }
function togglePause() {
  paused = !paused;
  document.getElementById('btn-pause').textContent = paused ? '▶' : '⏸';
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// OFFLINE SUPPORT
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
if(!navigator.onLine) {
  document.getElementById('offline-notice').style.display = 'block';
}
window.addEventListener('offline', function() {
  document.getElementById('offline-notice').style.display = 'block';
});
window.addEventListener('online', function() {
  document.getElementById('offline-notice').style.display = 'none';
});
document.addEventListener('error', function(e) {
  if(e.target.tagName === 'IMG' && !e.target.dataset.fixed) {
    e.target.dataset.fixed = '1';
    var src = e.target.getAttribute('src') || '';
    // Fix protocol-relative URL
    if(src.indexOf('//') === 0) {
      e.target.src = 'https:' + src;
      return;
    }
    // Show small placeholder only after fixing attempt
    e.target.style.cssText = 'max-width:100%;min-height:40px;background:#f5f5f5;border:1px dashed #ddd;border-radius:4px';
    e.target.alt = navigator.onLine ? 'Image failed to load' : 'Offline - image unavailable';
  }
}, true);

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
buildAll();
</script>
